					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p3 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p3 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p3 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
	} elif playercount 4 {
		if p1 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p1 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p1 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p2 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p2 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p3 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p3 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p3 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
		if p4 healthbelow 10% {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p4 health below 10%. Using potion..."
			}
			p4 usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if p4 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
	} else {
		if healthbelow 10% {
			usepotion
			call Buy_Potions_If_Needed
			if Resume_Dungeons_On_Death = True {
				if windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ] {
					clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'ResumeInstanceButton' ]
					call Load
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
block Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat {
	block Check_If_Died {
		if playercount 2 {
			if p1 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p1 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if Delay_Combat = True {
					until p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'QuitButton'] {
						p2 sendkey ESC, .5
					}
				}
				if p1 incombat {
					until NOT p1 incombat {
						p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					if Delay_Combat = True {
						until NOT p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'SettingPage', 'QuitButton'] {
							p2 sendkey ESC, .5
						}
					}
					return
				}
				until p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p2 healthbelow 1% {
				if p2 incombat {
					until NOT p2 incombat {
						p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
		} elif playercount 3 {
			if p1 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p1 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p1 incombat {
					until NOT p1 incombat {
						p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P3
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p2 healthbelow 1% {
				if p2 incombat {
					until NOT p2 incombat {
						p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p3 healthbelow 1% {
				if p3 incombat {
					until NOT p3 incombat {
						p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P3
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
		} elif playercount 4 {
			if p1 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p1 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p1 incombat {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p1 died but still in combat. Fleeing..."
					}
					until NOT p1 incombat {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Clicking Flee button to exit combat after death..."
						}
						p1 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p1 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P1 teleporting to help P4 after death..."
				}
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P4
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p2 healthbelow 1% {
				if p2 incombat {
					until NOT p2 incombat {
						p2 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p2 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				call Return_To_Booster_P2
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p3 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p3 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p3 incombat {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p3 died but still in combat. Fleeing..."
					}
					until NOT p3 incombat {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Clicking Flee button to exit combat after death..."
						}
						p3 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p3 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p3 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P3 returning to P1 after death recovery..."
				}
				call Return_To_Booster_P3
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
			if p4 healthbelow 1% {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "p4 has died! Handling death..."
				}
				if p4 incombat {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p4 died but still in combat. Fleeing..."
					}
					until NOT p4 incombat {
						if DebugMode = True {
							print "Clicking Flee button to exit combat after death..."
						}
						p4 clickwindow [ 'WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'Alignment', 'PlanningPhaseSubWindow', 'DoneWindow', 'DefeatedFleeButton' ]	
						p4 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'PlanningPhase', 'MessageBoxModalWindow', 'messageBoxBG', 'messageBoxLayout', 'AdjustmentWindow', 'Layout', 'centerButton']
					}
				} else {
					return
				}
				until p4 windowvisible [ 'WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook' ] {
					sleep .1
				}
				sleep 5
				call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P4 returning to P1 after death recovery..."
				}
				call Return_To_Booster_P4
				sleep 3
				times 3 {
					call Close_Menus
				}
			}
		}
	}		
	block All_Questing_Combat_Check {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = True {
			print "All clients questing. Checking combat..."
			if p1 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P1 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p1
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			} elif p2 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P2 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			} elif p3 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P3 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p3
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			} elif p4 incombat {
				if DebugMode = True {
					print "P4 is in combat. Attempting to bring other clients into combat..."
				}
				times 100 {
					if NOT incombat {
						tp XYZ(0,0,100000)
						tp p4
					} else {
						print "All clients in combat."
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	block Refresh_Health {
		times 4 {
			entitytp health
			entitytp mana
		}
		tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
		call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
	}
	if playercount 1 {
		if p1 incombat {
			p1 waitforcombat completion
			times 4 {
				entitytp health
				entitytp mana
			}
			tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
			call Extreme_Low_Health_Check
		}
	} elif playercount 2 {
		call All_Questing_Combat_Check
		if p2 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is in combat. P1 going to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p2 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p2 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p2 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p2
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p2 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p2
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
	} elif playercount 3 {
		call All_Questing_Combat_Check
		if p2 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is in combat. Attempting to bring clients to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p2 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p2 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p2 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p2
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p2 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p2
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
		if p3 incombat {
			if samezone and NOT except p3 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p3 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p3
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p3 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P3
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p3
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p3 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p3
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
	} elif playercount 4 {
		call All_Questing_Combat_Check
		if p2 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is in combat. Attempting to bring clients to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p2 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p2 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p2
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p2 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p2
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p2 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p2
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
		if p3 incombat {
			if samezone and NOT except p3 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p3 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p3
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p3 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P3
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p3
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p3 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p3
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
		if p4 incombat {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p4 is in combat. P1 going to help..."
			}
			if samezone and NOT except p4 incombat {
				times 50 {
					if NOT except p4 incombat {
						minustp XYZ(0,0,10000)
						tp p4
					} else {
						break
					}
					sleep .1
				}
			}
			if p1 incombat or p1 loading {
				times 10000 {
					if p1 incombat {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if NOT p1:p4 samezone {
				call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P4
			} else {
				call Close_Menus
				p1 tp p4
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT p4 incombat {
					break
				} else {
					if NOT except p1 samezone {
						call Check_If_Died
						sleep .1
					} else {
						call Check_If_Died
						tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
						sleep .1
						tp p4
						sleep .1
					}
				}
			}
			call Refresh_Health
		}
	}
}
block Debug_Quest_Checker {
	if DebugMode = True { 
		if any hasdialogue {
			times 10 {
				sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
			}
		}
		if playercount 1 {
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			logquest
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			loggoal
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			logzone
			if hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
		} else {
			except p1 logquest
			if any hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			except p1 loggoal
			if any hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
			except p1 logzone
			if any hasdialogue {
				times 10 {
					sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
block Check_If_On_Char_Screen {
	if Recconect_To_Game = True {
		if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'mainWindow', 'btnPlay'] and any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'mainWindow', 'btnNew'] {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print anyclientlist
				print "Client(s) in character selection. Attempting to press Play..."
			}
			times 3 {
				sameany clickwindow ['WorldView', 'mainWindow', 'btnPlay']
			}
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Pressed Play. Waiting for loading..."
			}
			times 10000 {
				if NOT sameany windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowQuickChat', 'btnHelpChat'] {
					sleep .1
				} else {
					sleep 2 # Additional 2 second sleep for extra safety
					return
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
block Check_If_Paused {
	if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'DeckConfigurationWindow', 'ControlSprite', 'DeckPage'] {
		print "Pausing auto-quester. To resume, press the key P or get off the spell deck page."
		times 1000 {
			if NOT hasxyz XYZ(0,100000,0) {
				tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
			} else {
				break
			}
			sleep .1
		}
		loop {
			if NOT sameany windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'DeckConfigurationWindow', 'ControlSprite', 'DeckPage'] {
				print "Resuming..."
				break
			}
			sendkey A, 1
		}
	}
}
block Watchdog_Check {
	# Detects if the bot is stuck by monitoring zone changes.
	# If any player changes zones, the bot is progressing - reset the counter.
	# If no zone change occurs over many iterations, trigger recovery.
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Watchdog: Checking for zone changes..."
	}
	if any zonechanged {
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Watchdog: Zone change detected. Resetting watchdog."
		}
		var WatchdogState = 0
		return
	}
	# No zone change - advance the state machine
	if WatchdogState = 0 {
		var WatchdogState = 1
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 1 {
		var WatchdogState = 2
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 2 {
		var WatchdogState = 3
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 3 {
		var WatchdogState = 4
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 4 {
		var WatchdogState = 5
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 5 {
		var WatchdogState = 6
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 6 {
		var WatchdogState = 7
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 7 {
		var WatchdogState = 8
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 8 {
		var WatchdogState = 9
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 9 {
		var WatchdogState = 10
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 10 {
		var WatchdogState = 11
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 11 {
		var WatchdogState = 12
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 12 {
		var WatchdogState = 13
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 13 {
		var WatchdogState = 14
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 14 {
		var WatchdogState = 15
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 15 {
		var WatchdogState = 16
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 16 {
		var WatchdogState = 17
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 17 {
		var WatchdogState = 18
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 18 {
		var WatchdogState = 19
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 19 {
		var WatchdogState = 20
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 20 {
		var WatchdogState = 21
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 21 {
		var WatchdogState = 22
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 22 {
		var WatchdogState = 23
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 23 {
		var WatchdogState = 24
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 24 {
		var WatchdogState = 25
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 25 {
		var WatchdogState = 26
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 26 {
		var WatchdogState = 27
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 27 {
		var WatchdogState = 28
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 28 {
		var WatchdogState = 29
		return
	}
	if WatchdogState = 29 {
		print "WATCHDOG TRIGGERED: No zone change detected. Initiating recovery..."
		call Close_Menus
		times 3 {
			tp XYZ(0,1000000,0)
		}
		times 3 {
			clickwindow ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'GoHomeButton'] 
		}
		print "Sleeping 30 seconds... The bot is not broken."
		sleep 30
		var WatchdogState = 0
		print "Watchdog: Recovery complete. Resuming normal operation."
		return
	}
}
block Orient_Edge_Cases {
	if any windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "sister constance" {
		if sameany inzone Avalon/AV_Z07_OuterYard {
			sameany turncam 2.11 
		} 
	}
	if any windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "warwick" {
		if sameany inzone Avalon/AV_Z07_OuterYard {
			sameany turncam 5.93
		} 
	}
	if any windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "merle ambrose" {
		if sameany inzone WizardCity/Interiors/WC_Headmistress_House {
			sameany turncam 3.35
		} 
	}
}

}
block Check_Network {
	if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'spriteNetworkLinkBroken'] {
		print "Network error detected. Waiting until network is stable..."
		times 1200 {     # Up to 2 minute wait time
			if any windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'spriteNetworkLinkBroken'] {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
	}
}
block Main_Quest_Handler {
	call Check_Network
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
	call Debug_Quest_Checker
	call Close_Menus
	block TP_All_Quest {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = True {
			if NOT sameplace {
				p1 tp quest
				sleep .5
				p2 tp quest
				sleep .5
				p3 tp quest
				sleep .5
				p4 tp quest
				sleep .5
			} else {
				p1 tp quest
				sleep .5
				tp p1
			}
		} else {
			if NOT sameplace {
				p2 tp quest
				sleep .5
				p3 tp quest
				sleep .5
				p4 tp quest
				sleep .5
				p1 tp quest
				sleep .5
			} else {
				tp quest
			}
		}
	}
	block Player_One_Handler {
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		call Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P1
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking broken quests and dungeons for P1..."
		}
		if NOT sameplace {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clients not in same place. Teleporting p1 to quest..."
			}
			call Close_Menus
			call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
			p1 tp quest
			call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		} else { 
			call Close_Menus
			call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
			call TP_All_Quest
			call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
		}
		times 20 {
			if NOT p1 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		if p1 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p1 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p1 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p1 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
					}
					sleep 1
				} else {
					break
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P1
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Checking X key type for P1..."
			}
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
	}
	if playercount 1 {
		call Player_One_Handler
	} elif playercount 2 {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = True {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "All accounts are questing. Handling P1..."
			}
			call Player_One_Handler
		}
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Main quest handler: playercount 2 mode active"
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P2
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking broken quests and dungeons for P2..."
		}
		if NOT sameplace {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clients not in same place. Teleporting p2 to quest..."
			}
			call Close_Menus
			p2 tp quest
		} else {
			call Close_Menus
			call TP_All_Quest
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
		}
		times 20 {
			if NOT p2 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		if p2 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p2 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
					}
					sleep 1
				} else {
					break
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P2
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Checking X key type for P2..."
			}
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking if any clients are in combat..."
		}
	} elif playercount 3 {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = True {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "All accounts are questing. Handling P1..."
			}
			call Player_One_Handler
		}
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Main quest handler: playercount 3 mode active"
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P2
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking broken quests and dungeons for P2..."
		}
		if NOT sameplace {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clients not in same place. Teleporting p2 to quest..."
			}
			call Close_Menus
			p2 tp quest
		} else {
			call Close_Menus
			call TP_All_Quest
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
		}
		times 20 {
			if NOT p2 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		if p2 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p2 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
					}
					sleep 1
				} else {
					break
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P2
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Checking X key type for P2..."
			}
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P3
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking broken quests and dungeons for P3..."
		}
		if NOT sameplace {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clients not in same place. Teleporting p3 to quest..."
			}
			call Close_Menus
			p3 tp quest
		} else {
			call Close_Menus
			call TP_All_Quest
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
		}
		times 20 {
			if NOT p3 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		if p3 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p3 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p3 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p3 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
					}
					sleep 1
				} else {
					break
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P3
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Checking X key type for P3..."
			}
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
	} elif playercount 4 {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = True {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "All accounts are questing. Handling P1..."
			}
			call Player_One_Handler
		}
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Main quest handler: playercount 4 mode active"
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P2
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking broken quests and dungeons for P2..."
		}
		if NOT sameplace {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clients not in same place. Teleporting p2 to quest..."
			}
			call Close_Menus
			p2 tp quest
		} else {

			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
		}
		times 20 {
			if NOT p2 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		if p2 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p2 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
					}
					sleep 1
				} else {

				}
			}
		} else {
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P2

		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat

		call Check_If_Paused
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat

		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen

		if DebugMode = True {

		if NOT sameplace {

		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen

	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Hub_Area_Check
}
block First_Run {
	print "Through the Spiral (Arc 2)"
	print "Created by: KamarJ"
	print "Last Updated: 07/15/2026"
	print "Requirements: Fullscreen (OFF) | Resolution: 800x600 | Combat (ON) | Speedhack (OFF) | Dialogue (ON) | Un-equip all homes before starting!"
	print "Enjoy!"
}
call First_Run
call First_Run_Settings

loop {
	call Check_Network
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Watchdog_Check
	call Quest_Check_Watchdog
	if Create_Adventure_Party = True {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = False {
			if Created_Party = False {
				if except p1 level > 29 {
					var Created_Party = True
					call Auto_Adventure_Party
				}
			}
		} else {
			print "Create adventure party and all questing can not both be true. Please fix and restart."
			kill
		}
	}
	call Sell_Inventory
	call Check_If_Paused
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Teleporting all clients to a safe area."
	}
	times 2 {
		tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
	}
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_Paused
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Checking if any account has dialogue..."
	}
	if any hasdialogue {
		times 10 {
			sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
		}
	}
	call Orient_Edge_Cases
	call Main_Quest_Handler
	call Orient_Edge_Cases
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Watchdog_Check
	call Quest_Check_Watchdog
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Waiting preset time..."
	}
	sleep $SpeedDelay
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_Paused
	if any hasdialogue {
		times 10 {
			sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
		}
	}
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Spell_Learner
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Check_Network
}
